# Apache Superset changelogs — 4.1.1 to 6.1.0

Compiled from upstream `CHANGELOG/*.md` on tag **6.1.0**.
Versions included, in release order: 4.1.1, 4.1.2, 4.1.3, 4.1.4, 5.0.0, 6.0.0, 6.1.0.
There is no 6.0.1 final changelog in that tag (only an RC existed).
